That is the only place where things are happening, because I can see the math changing. But outside of that? So, my certainty has stayed pretty much exactly the same the whole time.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a similar feeling of certainty about something being true that others didn't believe, outside of this work project?\n\nPatient: No, not really. Change my mind? No, I don't see how I could. I mean, that's really the whole reason I'm here.

I just know the \"what.\" The numbers don't add up the way I left them, and that's all the proof I need that they're doing it.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a period of time where you didn't believe the people on the project were tampering with your datasets?\n\nPatient: Well, yes. Before all this started, I didn't have any reason to think that. I'd say... the certainty that people on the project are tracking my revisions and planting these errors, that's been there for about the last two months. Before that, everything seemed fine. But for the past two months, it's been undeniable.\n\nDoctor: Have your thoughts about the people tampering with your datasets changed at all in intensity or certainty over the past two months?\n\nPatient: No, I wouldn't say they've changed. Um... once I saw the pattern about two months ago, I was sure of it. It hasn't really faded or gotten more intense since then. \n\nIt's just... it's just a constant fact now. I log in, I see the math has been altered, and I know they did it. So, my certainty has stayed pretty much exactly the same the whole time.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a similar feeling of certainty about something being true that others didn't believe, outside of this work project?\n\nPatient: No, not really. I mean... I've always just focused on my work, the numbers. The math is the math, you know? I haven't had anything else like this happen where people didn't believe me about something I knew was a fact. It's just this situation with the datasets.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt like you're being watched or followed, even when you're alone or there's no one around?\n\nPatient: No, I can't say that I have. Um... I've never really had any problems with people trying to hurt me or, you know, being out to get me. \n\nI've always just kept my head down and done my work. This whole thing with the datasets is... well, it's really the only time something like this has happened. Before this, nobody's ever tried to sabotage me or anything like that. So, no, nothing else comes to mind.\n\nDoctor: Do you think you will ever change your mind about the people tampering with your datasets?\n\nPatient: Change my mind? No, I don't see how I could. I mean, that's really the whole reason I'm here. \n\nI am pretty sure the people on that environmental modeling contract have been tracking my revisions. They're planting errors in the datasets to make it look like I can't lead the work anymore. My wife keeps trying to tell me it's just version-control mistakes. The junior analysts say the exact same thing... that it's just a versioning error in the system. \n\nBut I've been doing this a long time. I know what I calculated and I know what I entered. So, no, I'm not going to change my mind when I can see exactly what they're doing to my files.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever thought that there might be other people, outside of this project, who are against you or trying to harm you in some way?\n\nPatient: No, no one else. Um... like I was saying earlier, I've never really had issues with people being out to get me before this. \n\nIt's strictly the people on this specific contract. I log in, I see the math has been altered, and I know they did it. So, my certainty has stayed pretty much exactly the same the whole time.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a similar feeling of certainty about something being true that others didn't believe, outside of this work project?\n\nPatient: No, not really. I haven't had anything else like this happen where people didn't believe me about something I knew was a fact. But physically being watched? No. When I'm alone, I'm just alone.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had any other situations in your life where you felt someone was out to get you or hurt you in some way?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't. Um... I've never really had any problems with people trying to hurt me or, you know, being out to get me. \n\nI've always just kept my head down and done my work. This whole thing with the datasets is... well, it's really the only time something like this has happened. Before this, nobody's ever tried to sabotage me or anything like that. So, no, nothing else comes to mind.\n\nDoctor: Do you think you will ever change your mind about the people tampering with your datasets?\n\nPatient: Change my mind? No, I don't see how I could. I mean, that's really the whole reason I'm here. \n\nI am pretty sure the people on that environmental modeling contract have been tracking my revisions. They're planting errors in the datasets to make it look like I can't lead the work anymore. My wife keeps trying to tell me it's just version-control mistakes. The junior analysts say the exact same thing... that it's just a versioning error in the system. \n\nBut I've been doing this a long time. I know what I calculated and I know what I entered. So, no, I'm not going to change my mind when I can see exactly what they're doing to my files.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever thought that there might be other people, outside of this project, who are against you or trying to harm you in some way?\n\nPatient: No, no one else. They are still planting errors in the datasets to make it look like I can't lead the work anymore. My wife and the junior analysts keep telling me it's just version-control mistakes, but... well, they're wrong. I know what I'm looking at. So no, my certainty hasn't changed one bit.\n\nDoctor: Do you think it's possible that you might be mistaken about the people tampering with your datasets?\n\nPatient: No, I really don't think I'm mistaken. I mean, I know we keep going over this, but I'm looking right at the data. I can see where they've been tracking my revisions on the environmental modeling contract. They are planting those errors in the datasets, clear as day. It's all just to make it look like I'm incompetent and can't lead the work. My wife and the junior analysts... they keep trying to tell me it's just version-control mistakes, but I know what I'm seeing. So, no, I don't think it's possible I'm mistaken.\n\nDoctor: Have you considered the possibility that the errors in the datasets could be due to your own mistake?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't. I... It's not my mistake. \n\nThe people on that environmental modeling contract are tracking my revisions and putting those errors in the datasets on purpose. They just want it to look like I can't lead the work anymore. I know my wife and the junior analysts keep saying I probably just messed up the version control, but... no. I know what I'm doing, and I know it's not me making these mistakes.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had any other thoughts or beliefs that you were absolutely certain of, but which others did not believe, outside of your work or personal relationships?\n\nPatient: No, not really.
